Read in parallel (HANDOFF.md uses **read-from-end strategy** — SSP-01 v6.5.0; arquivo monolítico newest-first em ~3170+ linhas, leitura completa estoura limite de 25k tokens):
- `HANDOFF.md` with `limit=71` → Task State header + FIRST ACTION FOR NEXT AGENT (sempre estável, contém summary da fase atual selada)
- `HANDOFF.md` with `offset=<total_lines - 400>` `limit=400` → últimas 1-2 Records + Pending Actions + GraphRAG State + Infrastructure + Key Files (suffix sections always at the tail)
- `CLAUDE.md` (architecture rules, data flow, key files — incluindo MCORCH Master Execution Protocol + Survival Laws link)
- `.claude/context/sprint-priorities.md` (sprint goal, 4Cs snapshot, top gaps)
- `/home/ubuntu/.claude/projects/-home-gcrUX-htdocs-constellation-orchestra/memory/MEMORY.md` (memory index)

> ⚠️ **Auditoria histórica de seals antigos:** usar `Read` com offset arbitrário em HANDOFF.md (fora do fluxo padrão de pickup). Ordering newest-first: seal mais antigo ≈ linha 2805, seal mais recente logo após linha 71. Cada `## <Phase> Record (YYYY-MM-DD)` marca um seal. Manter o Read em chunks ≤ 400 linhas para preservar budget de tokens.

Also check for loose files in scratch/:
```bash
ls scratch/ 2>/dev/null && echo "⚠️ scratch/ has files — consider moving to .claude/scripts/db/" || echo "scratch/ clean ✅"
```

### BoK Gate check (v3 — MCORCH Master Execution Protocol)

For each BoK suite in `docs/bok/<slug>/`, verify completeness:
```bash
for slug in $(ls docs/bok/ 2>/dev/null); do
  required=(00-index 01-mrd 02-brd 03-prd 04-frd 05-sdd 06-data-model 07-process-flow 08-quality-metrics)
  missing=()
  for doc in "${required[@]}"; do
    [ -f "docs/bok/$slug/$doc.md" ] || missing+=("$doc")
  done
  [ ${#missing[@]} -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ $slug — BoK complete" || echo "⚠️ $slug — missing: ${missing[*]}"
done
```

If the user mentions working on a **new module / complex feature / integration / architectural change**:
- Check whether `docs/bok/<expected-slug>/` exists with all 9 docs.
- If missing: surface in the **ALERTAS** section as a critical block per CLAUDE.md MCORCH Master Execution Protocol.
- Recommend `/bok-scribe <idea summary>` before any code is written.

## Rules

- **TypeScript errors → report immediately.** If there are TS errors, list them under ALERTAS. Do not start implementing new features until errors are resolved (unless the user explicitly asks to proceed despite errors).
- **Unhealthy containers → report with triage.** Suggest the fix command if known (e.g., `docker compose restart mcorch-vector-engine` for chroma).
- **Uncommitted changes → describe, do not commit.** Only describe what exists in the working tree; committing is the user's call.
- **Keep the brief tight.** No padding. No repetition of CLAUDE.md architecture. The user already knows the architecture — they need the delta: what changed, what's broken, what's next.
- **Pending Actions priority order:** CRITICAL (before first client) > NON-CRITICAL > Phase Zeta > Core v3 > Epsilon v9.
- **Knowledge Mesh numbers:** use `GraphRAG State` section from HANDOFF.md as the source. Do not invent counts.

> 4973	### Embedding Model
> 4974	`openai/text-embedding-3-small`, `dimensions: 768` (truncated output via OpenRouter).

> 4976	
> 4977	### Embedding Pipeline (Autônomo — 2026-05-03)
> 4978	Três camadas eliminam intervenção manual:
> 4979	1. **INSERT trigger** — `trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert` (pg_net) dispara `embed-mcorch-node` assincronamente em todo INSERT onde `embedding IS NULL`. Nunca bloqueia o INSERT.
> 4980	2. **Post-commit hook** — `.git/hooks/post-commit` roda `node scripts/ingest-codebase.ts --quiet` em background após cada commit → re-ingesta AST.
> 4981	3. **MeshControlPanel** — UI bottom-left do MatrixPage: "Sync Embeddings" (`trigger-embeddings`) e "Refresh Mesh Stats" (`trigger-ingest`).
> 4982

> 5107	## Constellation Matrix Intelligence & Stabilization Record (2026-05-03)
> 5108	
> 5109	Sessão de elevação da `ConstellationMatrix` ao nível de **Code Intelligence** e estabilização do motor gráfico. O motor Sigma.js/ForceAtlas2 apresentava loops de renderização infinitos ("blinking") e resets de estado ao interagir com a sidebar. A correção envolveu a estabilização de referências de props, memoização rigorosa das opções do grafo e desacoplamento de filtros dos ciclos de renderização do canvas. Paralelamente, a Matrix foi dotada de inteligência de código nativa via hook `useNodeIntelligence` e um `NodeDetailsPanel` reestruturado com navegação por abas.

> 5112	|--------|--------|
> 5113	| `src/hooks/useNodeIntelligence.ts` — criado | ✅ Orquestração de busca de código (`mcorch_nodes`) + análise semântica simulada Gemini |
> 5114	| `src/components/orchestrator/NodeDetailsPanel.tsx` | ✅ Navegação por abas (Meta, Code, Brain) + Code Preview + IA Analysis flow |
> 5115	| `src/components/orchestrator/ConstellationMatrix.tsx` | ✅ Estabilização Sigma.js: memoized options + stable refs + engine integration fix |
> 5116	| `src/hooks/useConstellationSigma.ts` | ✅ `useRef` para event handlers + callbacks estáveis — fim do "blinking" |
> 5117	| `src/components/ui/progress.tsx` | ✅ Prop `indicatorClassName` adicionado para estilização dinâmica da barra de IA |

> 293	### Phase 5 — Quality & Autonomic Engineering (CMQ/OE BOK)
> 294	
> 295	**BoK lens:** CMQ/OE BOK (Certified Manager of Quality / Organizational Excellence)  
> 296	**Areas applied:**
> 297	- *Process Management* → control charts, process capability (Cp/Cpk targets)
> 298	- *Quality Tools* → FMEA for critical paths
> 299	- *Customer-Focused Org* → NPS/CSAT hooks
> 300	- *Organizational Excellence* → self-optimization loops
> 301	- *Continuous Improvement* → PDCA cycles for autonomic self-healing
> 302	
> 303	**Output — `08-quality-metrics.md` (Self-Optimization & Self-Healing Specification):**
> 304	```
> 305	Sections to generate:
> 306	  1. Quality Objectives (aligned to business objectives in BRD)
> 307	  2. KPI Dashboard Definition
> 308	     - KPI ID, Name, Formula, Target, Threshold (warn/critical), Data source
> 309	     - Supabase table/view that stores the metric
> 310	  3. FMEA Table (per critical FR)
> 311	     - Function, Failure Mode, Effect, Severity (1-10), Occurrence (1-10),
> 312	       Detection (1-10), RPN = S×O×D, Recommended Action
> 313	  4. Self-Optimization Loops
> 314	     - Trigger condition (metric threshold crossed)
> 315	     - Autonomous response (what the system does without human intervention)
> 316	     - Escalation path (when human decision is required)
> 317	  5. Self-Healing Specifications
> 318	     - Watchdog telemetry event types (new rows for infra_health_logs)
> 319	     - Auto-recovery script stubs (bash / Edge Function)
> 320	     - mega-brain-daemon integration points
> 321	  6. SLA / SLO / SLI Definitions
> 322	     - Service, SLI metric, SLO target (e.g. 99.5% uptime/month), SLA consequence
> 323	  7. PDCA Improvement Cadence
> 324	     - Review frequency, metrics owner, improvement backlog location
> 325	  8. Observability Checklist
> 326	     - Logging events (what, where, level)
> 327	     - Alerting rules (condition → channel → escalation)
> 328	     - Distributed tracing touchpoints
> 329	```
> 330	
> 331	---
> 332	
> 333	### Phase 6 — Cross-Document Traceability Validation
> 334	
> 335	**Actions (automated consistency check before writing `00-index.md` final status):**
> 336	
> 337	```
> 338	1. Build traceability chain:
> 339	   MR-NNN → BR-NNN → PR-NNN → FR-NNN → (DB table | Edge Function | Process)
> 340	
> 341	2. Assert:
> 342	   - No orphaned requirements (every ID appears in at least one downstream doc).
> 343	   - No undocumented Edge Functions (every function in SDD has JWT + secret listed).
> 344	   - No tables without RLS (every CREATE TABLE stub has RLS block).
> 345	   - mcoCoins cost defined for every AI-powered FR.
> 346	   - Every FMEA item with RPN > 100 has a recommended action.
> 347	
> 348	3. If any assertion fails:
> 349	   - List all failures with their document + section reference.
> 350	   - Ask Sovereign to resolve before proceeding to Phase 7.
> 351	   - Do NOT proceed to Phase 7 with unresolved critical failures.
> 352	```
> 353	
> 354	---
> 355	
> 356	### Phase 7 — Knowledge Mesh Injection
> 357	
> 358	> **Canonical pattern (sealed 2026-05-26, Sovereign directive):** every BoK seal MUST persist
> 359	> the documentation_suite node via a dedicated Bun-native seed script following the precedent
> 360	> `scripts/seed-openclaw-bok-seal.ts` (and its sibling `seed-gamificacao-nodes-bok-seal.ts`).
> 361	> Inline REST `curl` or one-off dashboard inserts are NOT allowed — the script form preserves
> 362	> reproducibility, version control, and material proof of the operation.
> 363	
> 364	**Required actions:**
> 365	
> 366	1. **Construct the payload** as a fully-typed object — placeholder example:
> 367	
> 368	```json
> 369	{
> 370	  "node_type": "documentation_suite",
> 371	  "name": "bok-scribe-seal-<projectSlug>-v1",
> 372	  "title": "<projectSlug> BoK Documentation Suite v1",
> 373	  "content": "<one-paragraph synthesis: idea, market, architecture, ID space summary, motivating context>",
> 374	  "tags": ["bok-scribe", "<projectSlug>", "<marketSegment>", ...domain-specific],
> 375	  "stability_score": 0.85,
> 376	  "revenue_impact": <estimated from BRD ROI section — 0 for internal enablers; non-zero only when direct revenue is documented>,
> 377	  "metadata": {
> 378	    "bok_version": "1.0.0",
> 379	    "documents": ["00-index","01-mrd","02-brd","03-prd","04-frd","05-sdd","06-data-model","07-process-flow","08-quality-metrics"],
> 380	    "document_lines": { ... per-file LOC + total },
> 381	    "phase_completed": "Phase 7",
> 382	    "traceability_validated": true,
> 383	    "traceability_orphans": 0,
> 384	    "id_space": { "MR": "MR-XX-001..NNN", "BR": "...", ... },
> 385	    "fmea_high_rpn_count": <int>,
> 386	    "fmea_high_rpn_items": ["FMEA-XX-NNN (RPN <value>)", ...],
> 387	    "new_migrations": <int>,
> 388	    "new_edge_functions": <int>,
> 389	    "integration_surface": "<standalone|extends-mcorch>",
> 390	    "regulatory_profile": "<LGPD|GDPR|financial|healthcare|none>",
> 391	    "monetization": "<direct|indirect|internal-enabler-zero-coin>",
> 392	    "derives_from_seal": "bok-scribe-seal-<parent-slug>-v1",
> 393	    "depends_on_sop": "<path to operational SOP if applicable>",
> 394	    "motivating_incident": "<incident reference if BoK was triggered by an incident>"
> 395	  }
> 396	}
> 397	```
> 398	
> 399	2. **Author a dedicated seed script** at `scripts/seed-<projectSlug>-bok-seal.ts` mirroring
> 400	   `scripts/seed-openclaw-bok-seal.ts` 1:1 in structure. The script MUST:
> 401	   - Use `@supabase/supabase-js` with `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` from `.env` (never hardcode).
> 402	   - INSERT the payload into `mcorch_nodes` using `.select('id, name').single()` for material proof.
> 403	   - INSERT a `DERIVES_FROM` edge in `mcorch_edges` pointing to the most semantically-related
> 404	     parent seal (typically the previous BoK seal in the topic family — discover via
> 405	     `SELECT id FROM mcorch_nodes WHERE name = 'bok-scribe-seal-<parent-slug>-v1'`).
> 406	   - Print the returned UUIDs to stdout for the agent to cite as material proof.
> 407	   - Be idempotent: if the seal already exists (UNIQUE name violation), log and exit 0 cleanly.
> 408	
> 409	3. **Execute the script** under the project venv:
> 410	   ```bash
> 411	   bun run scripts/seed-<projectSlug>-bok-seal.ts
> 412	   ```
> 413	   Capture the returned UUID + edge UUID. Confirm via REST:
> 414	   ```bash
> 415	   curl -s "$SUPABASE_URL/rest/v1/mcorch_nodes?id=eq.<uuid>&select=embedding" \
> 416	     -H "apikey: $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" \
> 417	     -H "Authorization: Bearer $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"
> 418	   # Expected: embedding NOT NULL (auto-fired via trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert pg_net)
> 419	   ```
> 420	
> 421	4. **Cite the material proof** in the `/handoff` seal:
> 422	   - Documentation suite UUID
> 423	   - DERIVES_FROM edge UUID
> 424	   - Embedding confirmation timestamp
> 425	
> 426	**Note:** The BoK Scribe Agent itself does NOT directly invoke Supabase via in-conversation
> 427	REST calls — it produces the seed script + payload, and the Sovereign (or agent executing on
> 428	behalf of the Sovereign with explicit approval) runs the script. The script form preserves
> 429	the audit trail of "what was inserted, when, by which version of which code."
> 430	
> 431	**Precedent inventory (mirror sources, in order of recency):**
> 432	- `scripts/seed-e2e-user-zero-bok-seal.ts` (this BoK — 2026-05-26)
> 433	- `scripts/seed-openclaw-bok-seal.ts` (openclaw — 2026-05-19)
> 434	- `scripts/seed-gamificacao-nodes-bok-seal.ts` (gamificacao-nodes — 2026-05-18)
> 435	- (older seals predate this script pattern and were inserted via inline REST — grandfathered)
> 436	
> 437	---
> 438	
> 439	## Harness Engineering Constraints (non-negotiable)
> 440	
> 441	These rules apply to every document the agent produces:
> 442	
> 443	| Constraint | Rule |
> 444	|------------|------|
> 445	| Code separation | FRD/SDD describe WHAT and WHY. HOW belongs in code, not in docs. |
> 446	| Hook-first | Every UI feature must describe its backing hook before its component. |
> 447	| No client secrets | SDD never lists secret values, only key names from the vault. |
> 448	| Atomic billing | mcoCoins deduction always routes through `deduct_mco_coins` RPC. |
> 449	| RLS by default | Every new table starts with `ALTER TABLE x ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`. |
> 450	| Embed on insert | Every new node-type table must have embed-mcorch-node trigger noted. |
> 451	| Tenant isolation | Every table must document its `user_id + project_id` scoping. |
> 452	
> 453	---
> 454	
> 455	## Error & Edge Case Handling
> 456	
> 457	| Situation | Agent Response |
> 458	|-----------|----------------|
> 459	| Business idea is too vague | Ask 3 clarifying questions before Phase 1. |
> 460	| Existing docs/bok/<slug>/ detected | Show index, ask: overwrite / extend / cancel. |
> 461	| Phase transition check fails | List failures, stop, ask Sovereign to resolve. |
> 462	| Conflicting BoK recommendations | Note the conflict in an `[OTD]` block; present both options. |
> 463	| Regulatory profile = financial/healthcare | Elevate all CISSP CBK sections; add dedicated compliance checklist. |
> 464	| Feature extends existing MCORCH feature | Cross-reference existing ADRs from `.claude/context/architecture-decisions.md`. |
> 465	
> 466	---
> 467	
> 468	## Document Style Guide
> 469	
> 470	- **Headings:** `#` for document title, `##` for major sections, `###` for subsections.
> 471	- **IDs:** Always prefix (MR-, BR-, PR-, FR-, NFR-, OTD-) and zero-pad to 3 digits.
> 472	- **Tables:** Use markdown tables with aligned columns; include a header row always.
> 473	- **Code blocks:** Use fenced blocks with language identifier (`sql`, `typescript`, `json`).
> 474	- **Status badges:** `[ ] pending` / `[x] complete` / `[!] blocked` in index file.
> 475	- **Cross-references:** Link within suite as `[BR-001](../02-brd.md#br-001)`.
> 476	- **Version:** Every file footer includes `_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_`.
> 477	
> ```

> 96	
> 97	## 4. API Contract Definitions
> 98	
> 99	> All Edge Functions must validate JWT before any business logic.
> 100	
> 101	### `POST /functions/v1/{{EdgeFn1}}`
> 102	
> 103	```typescript
> 104	// Request
> 105	interface {{EdgeFn1}}Request {
> 106	  {{REQ_FIELD_1}}: {{REQ_TYPE_1}}
> 107	  {{REQ_FIELD_2}}: {{REQ_TYPE_2}}
> 108	}
> 109	
> 110	// Response (HTTP 200)
> 111	interface {{EdgeFn1}}Response {
> 112	  status: 'ok'
> 113	  {{RESP_FIELD_1}}: {{RESP_TYPE_1}}
> 114	}
> 115	
> 116	// mcoCoins deduction point:
> 117	// → Line ~40: await supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins', { p_user_id, p_amount: {{COIN_AMOUNT}} })
> 118	// → HTTP 402 if insufficient balance
> 119	```
> 120	
> 121	---
> 122	
> 123	## 5. Security Design
> 124	
> 125	### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE)
> 126	
> 127	| Component | Threat | Type | Severity | Mitigation |
> 128	|-----------|--------|------|----------|------------|
> 129	| `{{EdgeFn1}}` | Unauthenticated access | Spoofing | High | JWT validation at entry point |
> 130	| `{{TABLE_1}}` | Cross-tenant data read | Information Disclosure | Critical | RLS `user_id = auth.uid()` |
> 131	| mcoCoins deduction | Client-side bypass | Tampering | Critical | Server-side RPC only |
> 132	| {{THREAT_4}} | {{THREAT_4_DESC}} | {{THREAT_4_TYPE}} | {{THREAT_4_SEV}} | {{THREAT_4_MIT}} |
> 133	
> 134	### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions
> 135	
> 136	```sql
> 137	-- {{TABLE_1}} RLS
> 138	ALTER TABLE {{TABLE_1}} ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
> 139	
> 140	CREATE POLICY "{{TABLE_1}}_select_own"
> 141	  ON {{TABLE_1}} FOR SELECT
> 142	  USING (user_id = auth.uid());
> 143	
> 144	CREATE POLICY "{{TABLE_1}}_insert_own"
> 145	  ON {{TABLE_1}} FOR INSERT
> 146	  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());
> 147	```
> 148	
> 149	### 5.3 JWT Validation Points
> 150	
> 151	All Edge Functions follow this pattern:
> 152	```typescript
> 153	const authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization')
> 154	if (!authHeader) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
> 155	const { data: { user }, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser(
> 156	  authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '')
> 157	)
> 158	if (error || !user) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
> 159	```
> 160

> 78	```

> 59	## 5. Self-Healing Specifications
> 60	
> 61	### 5.1 Watchdog Telemetry Events
> 62	
> 63	New event types to be written to `infra_health_logs` by this feature:

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/.claude/worktrees/youthful-poincare-565e7c/docs/bok/mercado-livre-api/01-mrd.md",
  "content": "# MRD \u2014 Market Requirements Document\n\n**Project:** mercado-livre-api\n**Version:** v0.1 | **Date:** 2026-05-30\n**BoK Applied:** BABOK v3 (Strategy Analysis + Elicitation & Collaboration)\n**Status:** Draft\n\n---\n\n## 1. Executive Summary\n\nO MCORCH gera conte\u00fado monetiz\u00e1vel e o distribui de forma aut\u00f4noma, mas a camada de **afilia\u00e7\u00e3o Mercado Livre** \u2014 o ponto onde o conte\u00fado vira receita \u2014 est\u00e1 materialmente quebrada: os links de afiliado apontam para IDs `MLB` placeholders fabricados (`link-forge.ts:54-74`) e roteiam via `panel.gcrux.com/api/ml-redirect`, um proxy legado que retorna 404. Nenhuma comiss\u00e3o \u00e9 atribu\u00edvel. Esta fam\u00edlia de integra\u00e7\u00e3o substitui esse caminho pela **API oficial do Mercado Livre** em tr\u00eas m\u00f3dulos soberanos e multi-tenant: **(A) ML Core Afiliados** (links reais `meli.la/...` via API oficial + tag `caga6077534`), **(B) Global Selling** (cat\u00e1logo cross-border em moeda forte), e **(C) Mercado Pago per-user** (gateway de pagamento com credenciais por usu\u00e1rio). O momento \u00e9 agora: o flywheel de conte\u00fado (`orchestrate-content`) j\u00e1 roda E2E e produz drafts; sem link de afiliado real, todo o valor gerado vaza no \u00faltimo metro.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Market Problem Statement\n\n### 2.1 Current State\n\nUm criador/afiliado que usa o MCORCH para publicar conte\u00fado monetizado hoje enfrenta:\n- **Links 404:** o cat\u00e1logo (`vm_affiliate_products`) e o `link-forge.ts` emitem URLs com IDs `MLB` fict\u00edcios \u2192 o clique do consumidor morre em p\u00e1gina inexistente. Zero convers\u00e3o poss\u00edvel.\n- **Atribui\u00e7\u00e3o inexistente:** sem a tag de afiliado oficial (`caga6077534`) injetada via API, nenhuma venda \u00e9 creditada \u00e0 conta do criador no programa de afiliados ML.\n- **Sem pagamento pr\u00f3prio:** n\u00e3o h\u00e1 como o criador receber pagamentos diretos (produtos pr\u00f3prios, infoprodutos) \u2014 n\u00e3o existe integra\u00e7\u00e3o Mercado Pago.\n- **Tenancy global fr\u00e1gil:** o que existe usa token global (`GCRUX_ML_AFFILIATE_TOKEN`, j\u00e1 removido em v6.14.0) \u2014 incompat\u00edvel com multi-tenant onde cada criador tem sua pr\u00f3pria conta de afiliado.\n\n### 2.2 Root Cause Analysis\n\n- **Technology gap:** nunca foi integrada a API oficial de afiliados do Mercado Livre; o `panel.gcrux.com` era um stub de desenvolvimento que virou d\u00edvida de produ\u00e7\u00e3o.\n- **Process gap:** gera\u00e7\u00e3o de link nunca passou por resolu\u00e7\u00e3o real de item (`/items/{id}`) nem por cria\u00e7\u00e3o de short link oficial \u2014 apenas concatenava strings.\n- **Market gap:** o programa de afiliados ML evoluiu para exigir links curtos rastre\u00e1veis (`meli.la`) com tag; abordagens de query-string manual n\u00e3o s\u00e3o mais atribu\u00edveis com confiabilidade.\n\n### 2.3 Desired State\n\nTodo conte\u00fado publicado pelo MCORCH carrega um link de afiliado **real, curto e atribu\u00edvel** (`meli.la/...`) gerado pela conta de afiliado **do pr\u00f3prio usu\u00e1rio** (resolu\u00e7\u00e3o per-user via `auth.uid()`), com a comiss\u00e3o fluindo de volta via postback (`handle-ml-postback` \u2192 `ATTRIBUTES_REVENUE_TO`) e vis\u00edvel no `ROIWidget`. Criadores com produto pr\u00f3prio recebem pagamentos via **seu pr\u00f3prio** Mercado Pago. Cat\u00e1logo Global Selling permite ofertas cross-border em moeda forte.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Target Market Segments\n\n| Segment | Description | Size Estimate | Urgency | Accessibility |\n|---------|-------------|---------------|---------|---------------|\n| Primary | Criadores de conte\u00fado afiliados ML (Usu\u00e1rio Zero \u2192 Usu\u00e1rio N) que monetizam tr\u00e1fego social via comiss\u00e3o | SOM inicial: 1 (Gabriel/Usu\u00e1rio Zero), escalando a centenas de criadores BR | High | Direta (j\u00e1 dentro do MCORCH) |\n| Secondary | Vendedores/infoprodutores que cobram via Mercado Pago e querem distribui\u00e7\u00e3o de conte\u00fado + checkout | SAM: milhares de PMEs/sellers BR | Medium | Via onboarding `/dashboard/settings` |\n\n### 3.1 TAM / SAM / SOM\n\n| Metric | Value | Basis |\n|--------|-------|-------|\n| TAM | ~R$ 200 Bi GMV/ano do Mercado Livre Brasil; programa de afiliados com comiss\u00f5es 1-8% | Mercado p\u00fablico ML BR |\n| SAM | Criadores de conte\u00fado BR monetizando afiliados ML (~centenas de milhares) | Estimativa do ecossistema afiliados BR |\n| SOM (12m) | Usu\u00e1rio Zero + primeiros 10-50 criadores piloto na plataforma MCORCH | Roadmap comercial v6.x \u2192 Phase Commercial |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Competitive Landscape\n\n| Competitor | Positioning | Strengths | Weaknesses | Our Differentiation |\n|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|---------------------|\n| Painel oficial Afiliados ML (web) | Gera\u00e7\u00e3o manual de link no site do ML | Oficial, confi\u00e1vel | Manual, 1 link por vez, sem automa\u00e7\u00e3o de conte\u00fado | MCORCH gera link **dentro do pipeline** de conte\u00fado, em escala, por tenant |\n| Linktree / Beacons + afiliado manual | Agregador de links | F\u00e1cil setup | N\u00e3o gera conte\u00fado, n\u00e3o atribui receita ao grafo | Integra\u00e7\u00e3o nativa conte\u00fado\u2192link\u2192postback\u2192ROI no Knowledge Mesh |\n| Hotmart / Eduzz (infoproduto) | Plataforma de infoproduto + checkout pr\u00f3prio | Checkout robusto, afilia\u00e7\u00e3o interna | Fechado, n\u00e3o cobre cat\u00e1logo ML f\u00edsico | MCORCH combina afilia\u00e7\u00e3o ML f\u00edsica + Mercado Pago pr\u00f3prio + Global Selling |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Market Opportunity & Timing\n\nPor que agora: (1) o flywheel de conte\u00fado MCORCH j\u00e1 est\u00e1 vivo (pipeline E2E selado em v6.8.2) \u2014 s\u00f3 falta o elo de monetiza\u00e7\u00e3o real; (2) a diretiva **API Tenancy Model** (v6.6.7) j\u00e1 estabeleceu o padr\u00e3o per-user que esta integra\u00e7\u00e3o exige; (3) a remo\u00e7\u00e3o dos tokens globais em v6.14.0 deixou o caminho fail-closed pronto para receber a API real; (4) Global Selling do ML abriu cross-border, permitindo receita em moeda forte \u2014 alinhado \u00e0 diretiva Sovereign de moeda forte.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Market Requirements\n\n> Priority: **Critical** (must have at launch) | **High** | **Medium** | **Low**\n\n| ID | Market Requirement | Priority | Rationale | Source |\n|----|-------------------|----------|-----------|--------|\n| MR-ML-001 | Links de afiliado ML reais e atribu\u00edveis (`meli.la/...`) gerados via API oficial com a tag do afiliado | Critical | Sem isso, zero receita; fecha o Afiliados bug 1 (404) | Sovereign dogfood v6.14.0 |\n| MR-ML-002 | Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o de credencial de afiliado **por usu\u00e1rio** (`auth.uid()`), nunca token global | Critical | Multi-tenant; atribui\u00e7\u00e3o n\u00e3o pode vazar entre criadores | API Tenancy Model (CLAUDE.md) |\n| MR-ML-003 | Atribui\u00e7\u00e3o de receita rastre\u00e1vel de volta ao tenant via postback | High | ROI real por criador; alimenta `ROIWidget` + `ATTRIBUTES_REVENUE_TO` | OE-661 ROADMAP |\n| MR-ML-004 | Gateway de pagamento pr\u00f3prio do criador (Mercado Pago per-user) | High | Permite receita direta de produto pr\u00f3prio, n\u00e3o s\u00f3 comiss\u00e3o | Sovereign directive (Mercado Pago per-user) |\n| MR-ML-005 | Cat\u00e1logo cross-border em moeda forte (Global Selling) | Medium | Receita em USD; diversifica exposi\u00e7\u00e3o cambial | Sovereign directive (moeda forte) |\n| MR-ML-006 | Auto-recupera\u00e7\u00e3o e telemetria (self-healing) em cada caminho de API externa | High | API externa falha; sistema n\u00e3o pode morrer silenciosamente | Survival Law 1 + infra_health_logs pattern |\n| MR-ML-007 | N\u00c3O integrar Mercado Envios | Critical | Fora de escopo \u2014 MCORCH n\u00e3o faz envio f\u00edsico | Sovereign directive (skip Envios) |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Success Metrics (Market-Level KPIs)\n\n| KPI | Definition | Target (6-month) | Target (12-month) | Measurement |\n|-----|-----------|------------------|-------------------|-------------|\n| Link Attribution Rate | % de links gerados que s\u00e3o `meli.la` reais (n\u00e3o placeholder) | 100% | 100% | `affiliate_links` schema audit |\n| Revenue Attribution | n\u00ba de tenants com `revenue_cents > 0` | \u2265 1 (Usu\u00e1rio Zero) | \u2265 10 criadores | `affiliate_links` + `handle-ml-postback` |\n| Mercado Pago Activation | n\u00ba de criadores com MP configurado e 1\u00aa cobran\u00e7a | 1 | \u2265 5 | `mercado_pago_config` |\n| Cross-tenant leak incidents | vazamentos de atribui\u00e7\u00e3o entre tenants | 0 | 0 | Security review + RLS audit |\n\n---\n\n## 8. Regulatory & Compliance Context\n\n| Regulation | Applicability | Key Constraint | Compliance Owner |\n|------------|---------------|----------------|-----------------|\n| LGPD | Sim \u2014 credenciais ML/MP s\u00e3o dados sens\u00edveis do titular | Credenciais per-user com RLS `auth.uid()`; usu\u00e1rio pode revogar/excluir | Engineering |\n| Financial (Mercado Pago / PCI-adjacent) | Sim \u2014 fluxo de pagamento | MCORCH N\u00c3O armazena dados de cart\u00e3o; usa OAuth/tokens MP; tokens server-side only | Engineering / Sovereign |\n| ML Developer Terms | Sim \u2014 uso da API oficial | Respeitar rate limits, escopos OAuth, e termos do programa de afiliados | Engineering |\n| ML Afiliados Program Terms | Sim \u2014 tag `caga6077534` | Tag deve pertencer ao afiliado correto; per-user no multi-tenant | Sovereign |\n\n---\n\n## 9. Traceability Matrix Stub\n\n| MR ID | \u2192 BR ID | Rationale |\n|-------|---------|-----------|\n| MR-ML-001 | BR-ML-001, BR-ML-002 | Gera\u00e7\u00e3o de link real \u00e9 a capacidade central |\n| MR-ML-002 | BR-ML-003 | Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o per-user de credencial |\n| MR-ML-003 | BR-ML-004 | Postback de receita |\n| MR-ML-004 | BR-ML-005, BR-ML-006 | Mercado Pago per-user |\n| MR-ML-005 | BR-ML-007 | Global Selling cross-border |\n| MR-ML-006 | BR-ML-008 | Self-healing + telemetria |\n| MR-ML-007 | BR-ML-009 | Exclus\u00e3o expl\u00edcita de Envios |\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

  "content": "# BRD \u2014 Business Requirements Document\n\n**Project:** mercado-livre-api\n**Version:** v0.1 | **Date:** 2026-05-30\n**BoK Applied:** BABOK v3 (Requirements Analysis & Design Definition)\n**Status:** Draft\n\n---\n\n## 1. Executive Summary\n\nEsta BoK governa a substitui\u00e7\u00e3o do caminho de afilia\u00e7\u00e3o quebrado por integra\u00e7\u00e3o com a **API oficial do Mercado Livre**, em tr\u00eas m\u00f3dulos: **(A) ML Core Afiliados**, **(B) Global Selling**, **(C) Mercado Pago per-user**. Todos seguem o **API Tenancy Model** (credencial per-user \u2192 fallback vault restrito \u2192 hard failure 402/501), self-healing via `infra_health_logs`, e atribui\u00e7\u00e3o de receita isolada por tenant. O resultado de neg\u00f3cio: fechar o \u00faltimo elo do flywheel \u2014 conte\u00fado que **realmente** converte em comiss\u00e3o atribu\u00edvel ao criador correto.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Business Objectives (SMART)\n\n| ID | Objective | Specific | Measurable | Achievable | Relevant | Time-bound |\n|----|-----------|----------|------------|------------|----------|------------|\n| BO-ML-001 | Eliminar links 404 | Substituir 100% dos links placeholder por `meli.la` reais | `affiliate_links` sem IDs MLB fabricados | Sim \u2014 API oficial + tabela j\u00e1 existe | Receita depende disso | Antes do Usu\u00e1rio 1 |\n| BO-ML-002 | Atribui\u00e7\u00e3o per-tenant | Cada link usa a credencial de afiliado do `auth.uid()` dono | 0 vazamentos cross-tenant em auditoria RLS | Sim \u2014 `affiliate_config` j\u00e1 per-user | Compliance + fraude | MVP |\n| BO-ML-003 | Receita real rastre\u00e1vel | \u2265 1 tenant com `revenue_cents > 0` originado de `meli.la` real | `ROIWidget` mostra receita > 0 | Sim \u2014 `handle-ml-postback` existe | Prova do flywheel | v1.0 |\n| BO-ML-004 | Pagamento pr\u00f3prio | Criador recebe \u2265 1 pagamento via seu Mercado Pago | `mercado_pago_config` ativo + 1 payment confirmado | Sim \u2014 MP OAuth Connect | Diversifica receita | v1.0 |\n| BO-ML-005 | Moeda forte | Cat\u00e1logo Global Selling exp\u00f5e \u2265 1 oferta em USD | `vm_affiliate_products` com currency=USD | Sim \u2014 Global Selling API | Hedge cambial | Future |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Stakeholder Register\n\n| ID | Role | Interest | Influence | Communication Cadence | Contact |\n|----|------|----------|-----------|----------------------|---------|\n| SH-ML-001 | Sovereign / Maestro (Gabriel) | High | High | Continuous | gabrielcall@gmail.com |\n| SH-ML-002 | Criador afiliado (Usu\u00e1rio Zero \u2192 N) | High | Medium | Onboarding + NPS | via `/dashboard/affiliates` |\n| SH-ML-003 | Consumidor final (clica o link) | Medium | Low | \u2014 (indireto) | tr\u00e1fego social |\n| SH-ML-004 | Mercado Livre (plataforma/API) | High | High | Termos + rate limits | developers.mercadolivre.com.br |\n| SH-ML-005 | Mercado Pago (gateway) | High | High | Webhooks + OAuth | MP Developers |\n| SH-ML-006 | Engineering (MCORCH Agent) | High | High | Por sess\u00e3o `/handoff` | \u2014 |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Business Requirements\n\n> Cada BR tra\u00e7a a \u2265 1 MR. mcoCoins definido quando h\u00e1 gera\u00e7\u00e3o/processamento custoso.\n\n| ID | Requirement | MR Traced | Priority | mcoCoins Cost | Acceptance Criteria |\n|----|-------------|-----------|----------|---------------|---------------------|\n| BR-ML-001 | Resolver produto ML real via `/items/{id}` ou `/sites/MLB/search` antes de gerar link | MR-ML-001 | Critical | 0 (lookup) | Item resolvido tem `id`, `permalink`, `price` reais |\n| BR-ML-002 | Gerar short link de afiliado oficial (`meli.la/...`) com tag via API ML Afiliados | MR-ML-001 | Critical | 1 mco/link | Link retornado come\u00e7a com `meli.la` e resolve 200 |\n| BR-ML-003 | Resolver credencial de afiliado por `auth.uid()` em `affiliate_config`; fallback vault s\u00f3 service-role | MR-ML-002 | Critical | 0 | POST user-facing nunca usa env global; hard fail 402 se n\u00e3o configurado |\n| BR-ML-004 | Postback de convers\u00e3o atualiza `affiliate_links.revenue_cents` + edge `ATTRIBUTES_REVENUE_TO` | MR-ML-003 | High | 0 | `revenue_cents > 0` aparece no tenant correto |\n| BR-ML-005 | Onboarding OAuth Mercado Pago per-user (Connect) com tokens server-side | MR-ML-004 | High | 0 | `mercado_pago_config` populado com `access_token` por user; RLS isolado |\n| BR-ML-006 | Criar prefer\u00eancia de pagamento MP e processar webhook de notifica\u00e7\u00e3o | MR-ML-004 | High | 0 | Payment `approved` \u2192 status persistido + telemetria |\n| BR-ML-007 | Buscar e exibir cat\u00e1logo Global Selling (cross-border, USD) | MR-ML-005 | Medium | 1 mco/refresh | `vm_affiliate_products` recebe linhas com `currency='USD'` |\n| BR-ML-008 | Telemetria `infra_health_logs` (healthy/degraded/error) em cada caminho de API ML/MP | MR-ML-006 | High | 0 | Cada fun\u00e7\u00e3o emite \u2265 1 pulse por invoca\u00e7\u00e3o |\n| BR-ML-009 | N\u00c3O implementar nenhum endpoint de Mercado Envios | MR-ML-007 | Critical | \u2014 | Aus\u00eancia verific\u00e1vel; documentado como out-of-scope |\n\n---\n\n## 5. ROI Model\n\n### 5.1 Cost Estimates\n\n| Cost Item | Type | Monthly Estimate | Annual Estimate |\n|-----------|------|-----------------|----------------|\n| Infraestrutura (Supabase, Docker) | OpEx | R$ 0 incremental (reusa stack) | R$ 0 |\n| API ML/MP calls | Variable | ~R$ 0 (APIs ML gratuitas; rate-limited) | ~R$ 0 |\n| Desenvolvimento (inicial) | CapEx | \u2014 | ~3-5 sess\u00f5es de engenharia |\n\n### 5.2 Revenue Streams\n\n| Stream | Model | Monthly Potential | Notes |\n|--------|-------|-----------------|-------|\n| Comiss\u00e3o de afiliado ML | Transaction (1-8% GMV) | Vari\u00e1vel por tr\u00e1fego do criador | N\u00facleo do flywheel |\n| Pagamento via Mercado Pago | Transaction (produto pr\u00f3prio) | Vari\u00e1vel | Receita direta do criador |\n| Global Selling | Transaction cross-border USD | Vari\u00e1vel | Hedge cambial |\n| mcoCoins (gera\u00e7\u00e3o de link/refresh) | Indirect (consumo de plano) | Liga ao ledger `profiles.mco_balance` | Custeia uso da plataforma |\n\n### 5.3 Payback Analysis\n\n| Metric | Value |\n|--------|-------|\n| Break-even (months) | Indireto \u2014 destrava receita do flywheel j\u00e1 constru\u00eddo |\n| 12-month ROI | Alto: o custo de n\u00e3o ter (links 404) \u00e9 receita = 0 |\n| IRR (estimated) | N/A formal \u2014 enabler cr\u00edtico de monetiza\u00e7\u00e3o |\n\n---\n\n## 6. mcoCoins Economics\n\n| Operation | Coins/Run | Plan Tier Mapping | Monthly Volume (est.) | Monthly Revenue (est.) |\n|-----------|-----------|-------------------|-----------------------|----------------------|\n| Gerar link de afiliado (`process-affiliate-link` POST) | 1 | Starter(500) / Pro(2000) / Ent(10k) | dezenas-centenas | consumo de saldo |\n| Refresh cat\u00e1logo Global Selling | 1 | idem | semanal | consumo de saldo |\n| Postback / OAuth / resolu\u00e7\u00e3o | 0 | \u2014 | \u2014 | \u2014 |\n\n> Dedu\u00e7\u00e3o sempre via `deduct_mco_coins` RPC at\u00f4mico. Nunca client-side.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Constraints & Assumptions\n\n### Constraints\n- API oficial ML exige OAuth 2.0 (app_id/client_secret) \u2014 j\u00e1 modelado em `affiliate_config`.\n- Mercado Pago exige conta de vendedor + app OAuth aprovado.\n- Rate limits do ML aplicam-se por app/token \u2014 necessidade de backoff e cache.\n- MCORCH N\u00c3O armazena dados de cart\u00e3o (PCI fora de escopo via tokeniza\u00e7\u00e3o MP).\n\n### Assumptions\n- A tag de afiliado `caga6077534` pertence \u00e0 conta do Sovereign (Usu\u00e1rio Zero) e ser\u00e1 per-user no multi-tenant.\n- O endpoint exato da API de cria\u00e7\u00e3o de short link de afiliado ser\u00e1 confirmado contra `developers.mercadolivre.com.br` (ver OTD-ML-001).\n- `affiliate_config`, `affiliate_links`, `vm_affiliate_products`, `handle-ml-postback` j\u00e1 existem e ser\u00e3o estendidos, n\u00e3o recriados.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Business Risk Register\n\n| Risk ID | Description | Probability | Impact | RPN | Mitigation |\n|---------|-------------|-------------|--------|-----|------------|\n| BR-RISK-ML-001 | Endpoint real de short link de afiliado diferente do assumido | 3 | 4 | 12 | OTD-ML-001 \u2014 validar contra docs oficiais antes de codar (Lei 1) |\n| BR-RISK-ML-002 | Vazamento de atribui\u00e7\u00e3o entre tenants (credencial errada) | 2 | 5 | 10 | RLS `auth.uid()` + resolu\u00e7\u00e3o per-user obrigat\u00f3ria + security-review |\n| BR-RISK-ML-003 | Rate limit / suspens\u00e3o do app ML por uso indevido | 3 | 4 | 12 | Backoff exponencial + cache + telemetria degraded |\n| BR-RISK-ML-004 | Token MP/ML expira e quebra fluxo silenciosamente | 3 | 4 | 12 | Refresh token + pulse `error` + alerta self-heal |\n| BR-RISK-ML-005 | Exposi\u00e7\u00e3o de `client_secret`/`access_token` no client | 2 | 5 | 10 | Tokens server-side only; column-level RLS; nunca no bundle |\n\n> Probability & Impact: 1=Low, 5=High. RPN = Probability \u00d7 Impact.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Business Acceptance Criteria\n\n| ID | Criterion | Verification Method |\n|----|-----------|-------------------|\n| BAC-ML-001 | Nenhum link gerado cont\u00e9m ID MLB fabricado | Audit `affiliate_links` + `link-forge.ts` sem hardcode |\n| BAC-ML-002 | Link `meli.la` real resolve HTTP 200/302 | `curl -I` material |\n| BAC-ML-003 | Credencial resolvida por `auth.uid()` | Code path review + RLS test cross-tenant |\n| BAC-ML-004 | `revenue_cents > 0` no tenant correto ap\u00f3s convers\u00e3o | REST query material |\n| BAC-ML-005 | Mercado Pago: 1 payment `approved` persistido | Webhook log + DB row |\n| BAC-ML-006 | Zero endpoint de Mercado Envios no c\u00f3digo | grep material aus\u00eancia |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Traceability Matrix\n\n| MR ID | BR ID | PR ID (stub) |\n|-------|-------|--------------|\n| MR-ML-001 | BR-ML-001, BR-ML-002 | _PRD_ |\n| MR-ML-002 | BR-ML-003 | _PRD_ |\n| MR-ML-003 | BR-ML-004 | _PRD_ |\n| MR-ML-004 | BR-ML-005, BR-ML-006 | _PRD_ |\n| MR-ML-005 | BR-ML-007 | _PRD_ |\n| MR-ML-006 | BR-ML-008 | _PRD_ |\n| MR-ML-007 | BR-ML-009 | _PRD_ |\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

  "content": "# PRD \u2014 Product Requirements Document\n\n**Project:** mercado-livre-api\n**Version:** v0.1 | **Date:** 2026-05-30\n**BoK Applied:** ProdBOK + CXBOK\n**Status:** Draft\n\n---\n\n## 1. Product Vision Statement\n\n> Para o **criador de conte\u00fado afiliado** que precisa **monetizar tr\u00e1fego social sem links quebrados nem trabalho manual**, a **Camada Mercado Livre API do MCORCH** \u00e9 uma **integra\u00e7\u00e3o soberana multi-tenant** que **transforma cada pe\u00e7a de conte\u00fado em um link de afiliado real, atribu\u00edvel e rastre\u00e1vel \u2014 com gateway de pagamento pr\u00f3prio**. Diferente do painel manual do Mercado Livre, nossa integra\u00e7\u00e3o gera o link dentro do pipeline de conte\u00fado, por tenant, e fecha o loop de receita no Knowledge Mesh.\n\n---\n\n## 2. User Personas\n\n### Persona 1 \u2014 Gabriel / \"O Soberano-Piloto\" (Primary)\n\n| Attribute | Detail |\n|-----------|--------|\n| Role | Sovereign + Usu\u00e1rio Zero \u2014 valida a plataforma como primeiro criador real |\n| Goals | Ver receita real (`revenue_cents > 0`) fluindo de conte\u00fado gerado pelo MCORCH; provar o flywheel ponta a ponta |\n| Frustrations | Links 404 que matam a convers\u00e3o; atribui\u00e7\u00e3o que n\u00e3o chega; ter que gerar link manualmente no site do ML |\n| Tech-savviness | 5 (expert / arquiteto) |\n| MCORCH usage pattern | Di\u00e1rio; dispara campanhas, monitora ROIWidget, audita mesh |\n| Quote | _\"N\u00e3o quero poesia de deploy \u2014 quero a comiss\u00e3o pingando na conta certa.\"_ |\n\n### Persona 2 \u2014 \"A Criadora Escalada\" (Secondary)\n\n| Attribute | Detail |\n|-----------|--------|\n| Role | Criadora de conte\u00fado BR que entra como Usu\u00e1rio 1..N quando a plataforma abrir |\n| Goals | Conectar a pr\u00f3pria conta de afiliado ML + Mercado Pago e receber comiss\u00e3o/pagamento sem tocar em c\u00f3digo |\n| Frustrations | Setup t\u00e9cnico de API; medo de configurar credencial errada; n\u00e3o saber se est\u00e1 atribuindo certo |\n| Tech-savviness | 2-3 (usa ferramentas no-code) |\n| MCORCH usage pattern | Onboarding via `/dashboard/affiliates` + `/dashboard/settings`; uso semanal |\n| Quote | _\"Eu s\u00f3 quero colar minha tag e ver funcionar.\"_ |\n\n---\n\n## 3. User Journey Maps\n\n### Journey: Gerar conte\u00fado monetizado (Persona 1, Happy Path)\n\n| Stage | Action | Touchpoint | Emotion | Opportunity |\n|-------|--------|-----------|---------|-------------|\n| Awareness | Configura credencial de afiliado ML | `/dashboard/affiliates` | \ud83d\ude10 Neutral | Valida\u00e7\u00e3o inline da credencial |\n| Consideration | Dispara campanha de conte\u00fado | `/dashboard/orchestration` | \ud83e\udd14 Curious | Preview do link que ser\u00e1 gerado |\n| Activation | Pipeline resolve produto + gera `meli.la` real | `process-affiliate-link` | \ud83d\ude0a Excited | Toast \"Link de afiliado gerado\" |\n| Value | Consumidor clica \u2192 compra \u2192 postback | `handle-ml-postback` | \ud83d\ude80 Delighted | `revenue_cents` atualiza ao vivo |\n| Retention | V\u00ea ROI acumulado por campanha | `ROIWidget` | \u2764\ufe0f Loyal | Insight: melhor produto/nicho |\n\n#### Edge Case 1: Credencial de afiliado n\u00e3o configurada\n> POST `process-affiliate-link` resolve `affiliate_config` por `auth.uid()` e n\u00e3o acha \u2192 HTTP 402 `{ error: \"ml_affiliate_not_configured\", action: \"Configure suas credenciais em /dashboard/affiliates\" }`. Fail-closed, nunca cai em token global.\n\n#### Edge Case 2: Produto ML inexistente / fora de estoque\n> `/items/{id}` retorna 404/closed \u2192 n\u00e3o gera link quebrado; retorna 422 com mensagem PT-BR e sugere busca por keyword via `/sites/MLB/search`.\n\n### Journey: Receber pagamento pr\u00f3prio (Persona 2, Happy Path)\n\n| Stage | Action | Touchpoint | Emotion | Opportunity |\n|-------|--------|-----------|---------|-------------|\n| Awareness | Conecta Mercado Pago (OAuth Connect) | `/dashboard/settings` | \ud83d\ude10 | Bot\u00e3o \"Conectar Mercado Pago\" |\n| Activation | Cria link de cobran\u00e7a / prefer\u00eancia | hook `useMercadoPago` | \ud83d\ude0a | QR/checkout pronto |\n| Value | Cliente paga \u2192 webhook `approved` | `mercado-pago-webhook` | \ud83d\ude80 | Notifica\u00e7\u00e3o PT-BR de pagamento |\n| Retention | Hist\u00f3rico de pagamentos | painel MP | \u2764\ufe0f | Reconcilia\u00e7\u00e3o autom\u00e1tica |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Feature Inventory (MoSCoW)\n\n> Effort: S=hours, M=days, L=week, XL=sprint\n\n### Must Have (MVP \u2014 M\u00f3dulo A: ML Core Afiliados)\n\n| ID | Feature | Persona | BR Traced | Effort | mcoCoins/run | Notes |\n|----|---------|---------|-----------|--------|-------------|-------|\n| PR-ML-001 | Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o real de item ML (`/items/{id}` + search) | P1 | BR-ML-001 | M | 0 | Estende `process-affiliate-link` |\n| PR-ML-002 | Gera\u00e7\u00e3o de short link de afiliado oficial (`meli.la`) | P1 | BR-ML-002 | L | 1 | N\u00facleo \u2014 fecha bug 404 |\n| PR-ML-003 | Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o de credencial per-user fail-closed | P1, P2 | BR-ML-003 | S | 0 | J\u00e1 parcialmente em v6.14.0 |\n| PR-ML-004 | Postback de receita \u2192 `ATTRIBUTES_REVENUE_TO` | P1 | BR-ML-004 | M | 0 | Estende `handle-ml-postback` |\n| PR-ML-005 | Remover IDs MLB placeholder de `link-forge.ts` / cat\u00e1logo | P1 | BR-ML-001 | S | 0 | Substitui hardcode por resolu\u00e7\u00e3o real |\n\n### Should Have (v1.0 \u2014 M\u00f3dulo C: Mercado Pago)\n\n| ID | Feature | Persona | BR Traced | Effort | mcoCoins/run | Notes |\n|----|---------|---------|-----------|--------|-------------|-------|\n| PR-ML-010 | OAuth Connect Mercado Pago per-user | P2 | BR-ML-005 | L | 0 | Nova tabela `mercado_pago_config` |\n| PR-ML-011 | Criar prefer\u00eancia de pagamento + webhook | P2 | BR-ML-006 | L | 0 | Edge fn `mercado-pago-webhook` |\n| PR-ML-012 | UI de conex\u00e3o MP em `/dashboard/settings` | P2 | BR-ML-005 | M | 0 | Hook `useMercadoPago` |\n\n### Could Have (Future \u2014 M\u00f3dulo B: Global Selling)\n\n| ID | Feature | Persona | BR Traced | Effort | mcoCoins/run | Notes |\n|----|---------|---------|-----------|--------|-------------|-------|\n| PR-ML-020 | Cat\u00e1logo Global Selling cross-border (USD) | P1 | BR-ML-007 | L | 1 | Estende `vm_affiliate_products` com currency |\n| PR-ML-021 | Filtro de moeda forte no cat\u00e1logo | P1 | BR-ML-007 | M | 0 | UI em `AffiliateProductsPage` |\n\n### Won't Have (This Cycle)\n\n- Mercado Envios (c\u00e1lculo de frete, etiquetas, rastreio) \u2014 **explicitamente fora de escopo** (MR-ML-007).\n- Gest\u00e3o de estoque/an\u00fancios pr\u00f3prios no ML (seller central).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Release Phasing\n\n### MVP Scope\n**Target date:** pr\u00f3xima sess\u00e3o de execu\u00e7\u00e3o p\u00f3s-BoK\n**Included:** PR-ML-001..005 (M\u00f3dulo A completo)\n**Success gate:** 1 link `meli.la` real gerado por `auth.uid()` + resolve 200 + `revenue_cents` pronto para receber postback\n\n### v1.0 Scope\n**Target date:** ap\u00f3s valida\u00e7\u00e3o do MVP por Usu\u00e1rio Zero\n**Adds:** PR-ML-010..012 (Mercado Pago per-user)\n**Success gate:** 1 pagamento MP `approved` persistido no tenant correto\n\n### Future Scope\n**Items:** PR-ML-020, PR-ML-021 (Global Selling)\n**Condition:** quando houver demanda de receita cross-border / moeda forte\n\n---\n\n## 6. Luxury UX Specifications\n\n### 6.1 Color & Visual Identity\n\n| Token | Value | Usage |\n|-------|-------|-------|\n| `--bg-deep` | `#0A0A0F` | Canvas background dos pain\u00e9is afiliado/MP |\n| `--accent-violet` | `#7C3AED` | CTA \"Gerar link\", \"Conectar Mercado Pago\" |\n| `--accent-cyan` | `#06B6D4` | Destaque de receita / ROI |\n| `--glass-surface` | `rgba(255,255,255,0.04)` | Cards de produto/link |\n| `--ml-yellow` | `#FFE600` | Acento sutil ML (badge, \u00edcone) \u2014 usar com parcim\u00f4nia |\n\n### 6.2 Animation Guidelines\n\n| Element | Library | Duration | Easing |\n|---------|---------|----------|--------|\n| Gera\u00e7\u00e3o de link (loading) | Framer Motion | 300ms | `easeInOut` |\n| Toast de sucesso/erro | sonner | 150ms | `easeOut` |\n| Badge \ud83d\udcb0 ML ao monetizar | CSS keyframes | 2s | `ease-in-out` |\n\n### 6.3 Interaction Patterns\n\n- **Cards de produto:** glass morphism; badge de moeda (BRL/USD); pre\u00e7o real resolvido.\n- **Bot\u00e3o \"Conectar Mercado Pago\":** gradient border; abre fluxo OAuth em nova aba.\n- **Estado de credencial ausente:** banner inline PT-BR com link para `/dashboard/affiliates` (nunca falha silenciosa).\n- **Toasts:** sucesso = violet, erro = vermelho, info = cyan.\n\n### 6.4 Accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AA)\n\n- Contraste \u2265 4.5:1 (aten\u00e7\u00e3o ao `--ml-yellow` sobre fundo escuro \u2014 usar s\u00f3 para \u00edcone, n\u00e3o texto).\n- Bot\u00f5es de OAuth keyboard-naveg\u00e1veis; ARIA labels nos \u00edcones.\n- Foco vis\u00edvel \u2265 2px.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Acceptance Criteria (Gherkin)\n\n```gherkin\nFeature: Gera\u00e7\u00e3o de link de afiliado Mercado Livre real\n\n  Scenario: Gerar link meli.la com credencial per-user (PR-ML-002, PR-ML-003)\n    Given um usu\u00e1rio autenticado com affiliate_config ativo para platform='mercadolivre'\n    And uma URL de produto ML v\u00e1lida\n    When ele aciona POST process-affiliate-link\n    Then o sistema resolve o item real via /items/{id}\n    And gera um short link oficial come\u00e7ando com \"meli.la\"\n    And persiste em affiliate_links com o user_id correto\n    And deduz 1 mcoCoin via deduct_mco_coins RPC\n\n  Scenario: Credencial de afiliado ausente (Edge Case 1)\n    Given um usu\u00e1rio autenticado SEM affiliate_config\n    When ele aciona POST process-affiliate-link\n    Then o sistema retorna HTTP 402\n    And o corpo cont\u00e9m { error: \"ml_affiliate_not_configured\" }\n    And N\u00c3O usa nenhum token global de fallback\n\n  Scenario: Conectar Mercado Pago per-user (PR-ML-010)\n    Given um usu\u00e1rio autenticado em /dashboard/settings\n    When ele completa o OAuth Connect do Mercado Pago\n    Then mercado_pago_config recebe o access_token sob o user_id dele\n    And a credencial \u00e9 invis\u00edvel a outros tenants (RLS auth.uid())\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Traceability Matrix\n\n| MR ID | BR ID | PR ID |\n|-------|-------|-------|\n| MR-ML-001 | BR-ML-001, BR-ML-002 | PR-ML-001, PR-ML-002, PR-ML-005 |\n| MR-ML-002 | BR-ML-003 | PR-ML-003 |\n| MR-ML-003 | BR-ML-004 | PR-ML-004 |\n| MR-ML-004 | BR-ML-005, BR-ML-006 | PR-ML-010, PR-ML-011, PR-ML-012 |\n| MR-ML-005 | BR-ML-007 | PR-ML-020, PR-ML-021 |\n| MR-ML-007 | BR-ML-009 | (Won't Have \u2014 Envios) |\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"
}

Scope Statement\n\nEste documento especifica os requisitos funcionais e n\u00e3o-funcionais de `mercado-livre-api`,\nderivado do PRD `03-prd.md` e alimentando o SDD `05-sdd.md`.\n\n**In scope:** resolu\u00e7\u00e3o real de item ML, gera\u00e7\u00e3o de short link de afiliado oficial (`meli.la`) per-user, postback de receita, OAuth Mercado Pago per-user + webhook, cat\u00e1logo Global Selling (USD).\n**Out of scope:** Mercado Envios (frete, etiquetas, rastreio); seller central (gest\u00e3o de an\u00fancios pr\u00f3prios); armazenamento de dados de cart\u00e3o.\n\n---\n\n## 2. persistir em `affiliate_links` | Critical | PR-ML-002 | EdgeFunction | AT-ML-002 |\n| FR-ML-003 | Resolver credencial per-user | Ler `affiliate_config` por `user_id = auth.uid()` + `is_active`; fallback vault s\u00f3 service-role; hard fail 402 | Critical | PR-ML-003 | EdgeFunction | AT-ML-003 |\n| FR-ML-004 | Deduzir mcoCoins at\u00f4mico | `deduct_mco_coins(p_user_id, 1)` antes de gerar link; 402 se saldo insuficiente | High | PR-ML-002 | EdgeFunction | AT-ML-004 |\n| FR-ML-005 | Postback de receita | `handle-ml-postback` mapeia convers\u00e3o \u2192 `affiliate_links.revenue_cents` + insere edge `ATTRIBUTES_REVENUE_TO` | High | PR-ML-004 | EdgeFunction | AT-ML-005 |\n| FR-ML-006 | Remover placeholders MLB | `link-forge.ts` e cat\u00e1logo deixam de emitir IDs fabricados; resolvem via FR-ML-001 | Critical | PR-ML-005 | Hook/Script | AT-ML-006 |\n| FR-ML-007 | OAuth Connect Mercado Pago | Fluxo OAuth MP per-user; troca code\u2192token; persiste em `mercado_pago_config` | High | PR-ML-010 | EdgeFunction | AT-ML-007 |\n| FR-ML-008 | Criar prefer\u00eancia de pagamento MP | Edge fn cria preference/checkout via MP API usando token per-user | High | PR-ML-011 | EdgeFunction | AT-ML-008 |\n| FR-ML-009 | Webhook de notifica\u00e7\u00e3o MP | `mercado-pago-webhook` valida assinatura, processa `payment.approved`, persiste status | High | PR-ML-011 | EdgeFunction | AT-ML-009 |\n| FR-ML-010 | UI conex\u00e3o Mercado Pago | Bot\u00e3o + estado em `/dashboard/settings` via `useMercadoPago` | Medium | PR-ML-012 | UI/Hook | AT-ML-010 |\n| FR-ML-011 | Cat\u00e1logo Global Selling | Buscar ofertas cross-border (USD) e popular `vm_affiliate_products` com `currency` | Medium | PR-ML-020 | EdgeFunction | AT-ML-011 |\n| FR-ML-012 | Telemetria em todos os caminhos | `pulse()` para `infra_health_logs` (healthy/degraded/error) em cada fun\u00e7\u00e3o | High | (transversal) | EdgeFunction | AT-ML-012 |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Non-Functional Requirements\n\n| ID | Category | Requirement | Target | Measurement |\n|----|----------|-------------|--------|-------------|\n| NFR-ML-001 | Performance | Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o de item + gera\u00e7\u00e3o de link p95 | < 1500ms | Supabase logs |\n| NFR-ML-002 | Performance | Postback de webhook p95 | < 800ms | Supabase logs |\n| NFR-ML-003 | Scalability | Links gerados sem degrada\u00e7\u00e3o | \u2265 100 concorrentes | Load test |\n| NFR-ML-004 | Availability | Uptime das edge fns ML/MP | 99.5%/m\u00eas | Watchdog telemetry |\n| NFR-ML-005 | Security | Endpoints user-facing exigem JWT v\u00e1lido | 100% | Security review |\n| NFR-ML-006 | Security | Tokens (`access_token`, `client_secret`) nunca no client bundle | 100% | Bundle audit + column RLS |\n| NFR-ML-007 | Reliability | Backoff exponencial em rate limit ML/MP | 3 tentativas (2/4/8s) | Code review |\n| NFR-ML-008 | Tenancy | 0 vazamento de credencial/atribui\u00e7\u00e3o cross-tenant | 0 incidentes | RLS test + security-review |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Interface Requirements\n\n### 4.1 UI \u2192 Hook \u2192 Edge Function Contract\n\n```\n[Component: AffiliateProductsPage / SettingsPage]\n  \u2193 calls\n[Hook: useAffiliateProducts / useMercadoPago / useUserApiKeys]\n  \u2193 calls (for sensitive ops)\n[Edge Function: process-affiliate-link / mercado-pago-* / handle-ml-postback]\n  \u2193 reads/writes\n[Supabase: affiliate_config \u00b7 affiliate_links \u00b7 vm_affiliate_products \u00b7 mercado_pago_config]\n```\n\n### 4.2 External API Contracts\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/process-affiliate-link` (estendida)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope)\n**Request:**\n```typescript\n{\n  product_url?: string,   // URL de produto ML a resolver\n  item_id?: string,       // alternativa: MLB id real\n  campaign_id: string,    // para UTM + tracking\n  content_id?: string,    // v\u00ednculo com content_library\n}\n```\n**Response (200):**\n```typescript\n{\n  status: \"ok\",\n  item_id: string,          // MLB real resolvido\n  affiliate_url: string,    // meli.la/... real\n  original_url: string,\n  link_id: string,          // uuid em affiliate_links\n}\n```\n**Error codes:**\n| Code | Condition |\n|------|-----------|\n| 401 | JWT ausente/ inv\u00e1lido |\n| 402 | `ml_affiliate_not_configured` OU saldo mcoCoins insuficiente |\n| 422 | Produto inexistente/fechado, ou URL inv\u00e1lida |\n| 429 | Rate limit ML (ap\u00f3s backoff) |\n| 500 | Erro interno (logado em `infra_health_logs`) |\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/mercado-pago-oauth` (nova)\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT (user scope)\n**Request:** `{ code: string, redirect_uri: string }`\n**Response (200):** `{ status: \"ok\", connected: true }`\n**Errors:** 401 (JWT) \u00b7 422 (code inv\u00e1lido) \u00b7 502 (MP OAuth falhou).\n\n#### `POST /functions/v1/mercado-pago-webhook` (nova, service-role/trigger)\n\n**Auth:** assinatura MP (x-signature) \u2014 N\u00c3O JWT de user (chamada externa).\n**Request:** payload de notifica\u00e7\u00e3o MP (`{ type, data: { id } }`).\n**Response (200):** `{ received: true }` (sempre 200 r\u00e1pido; processamento ass\u00edncrono).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Data Requirements\n\n| Field | Type | Nullable | Validation Rule | Error Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|------|----------|-----------------|----------------------|\n| `product_url` | text | sim* | URL v\u00e1lida de dom\u00ednio mercadolivre.com.br/mercadolibre.com | \"URL de produto Mercado Livre inv\u00e1lida.\" |\n| `item_id` | text | sim* | regex `^MLB\\d{6,}$` (item real) | \"ID de item Mercado Livre inv\u00e1lido.\" |\n| `affiliate_url` | text | n\u00e3o | deve iniciar com `meli.la` ou dom\u00ednio oficial de short link | \"Falha ao gerar link de afiliado real.\" |\n| `affiliate_config.app_id` | text | n\u00e3o | n\u00e3o-vazio quando `is_active` | \"Configure suas credenciais de afiliado.\" |\n| `mercado_pago_config.access_token` | text | n\u00e3o | token MP v\u00e1lido (server-side) | \"Conex\u00e3o com Mercado Pago expirada \u2014 reconecte.\" |\n\n> *`product_url` OU `item_id` obrigat\u00f3rio (ao menos um).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Test Strategy Matrix\n\n| FR ID | Unit Test | Integration Test | E2E Test | Notes |\n|-------|-----------|-----------------|----------|-------|\n| FR-ML-001 | \u2705 | \u2705 | \u2014 | Mock `/items/{id}` + fixture real |\n| FR-ML-002 | \u2705 | \u2705 | \u2705 | E2E: link `meli.la` resolve 200 |\n| FR-ML-003 | \u2705 | \u2705 | \u2014 | Cross-tenant RLS negativa |\n| FR-ML-004 | \u2705 | \u2705 | \u2014 | Saldo insuficiente \u2192 402 |\n| FR-ML-005 | \u2705 | \u2705 | \u2014 | Postback \u2192 `revenue_cents` no tenant correto |\n| FR-ML-006 | \u2705 | \u2014 | \u2014 | grep aus\u00eancia de MLB hardcode |\n| FR-ML-007 | \u2705 | \u2705 | \u2014 | OAuth code\u2192token (mock MP) |\n| FR-ML-008 | \u2705 | \u2705 | \u2014 | Preference criada |\n| FR-ML-009 | \u2705 | \u2705 | \u2014 | Assinatura inv\u00e1lida \u2192 reject |\n| FR-ML-012 | \u2705 | \u2014 | \u2014 | Pulse emitido em cada path |\n\n**Test files location:**\n- Unit: `src/hooks/__tests__/useMercadoPago.test.ts`, `useAffiliateProducts.test.ts`\n- Integration: `src/__tests__/integration/mercado-livre-api.test.ts`\n- E2E: `e2e/affiliate-link-generation.spec.ts`\n\n---\n\n## 7. `MP_WEBHOOK_SECRET` |\n| `global-selling-sync` (novo) | `global-selling-sync/index.ts` | JWT (user) | 1 | \u2014 (token ML per-user) |\n\n### 2.4 Zustand Store Slices\n\n| Slice | Store file | State `revenue_cents` | \u2014 |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Data Flow Diagrams\n\n### Use Case: Gerar link de afiliado real (FR-ML-001..004)\n\n```\nUser            Hook                  process-affiliate-link        ML API           DB\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502                         \u2502              \u2502\n \u2502\u2500[monetizar]\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                          \u2502                         \u2502              \u2502\n \u2502               \u2502\u2500[POST JWT {url}]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502                         \u2502              \u2502\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502\u2500[resolve affiliate_config per auth.uid()]\u2502\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502  (402 se ausente \u2014 fail-closed)         \u2502\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502\u2500[deduct_mco_coins(1)]\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 (402 se < 1)  \u2502\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502\u2500[GET /items/{id}]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502              \u2502\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502\u25c0\u2500[item real]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502              \u2502\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502\u2500[POST afiliados link]\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 (meli.la)    \u2502\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502\u25c0\u2500[short link]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502              \u2502\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502\u2500[INSERT affiliate_links]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502\n \u2502               \u2502                          \u2502\u2500[pulse infra_health_logs healthy]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502\n \u2502               \u2502\u25c0\u2500[200 {affiliate_url}]\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2502                         \u2502              \u2502\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500[badge \ud83d\udcb0 ML]\u2502                          \u2502                         \u2502              \u2502\n```\n\n### Use Case: Pagamento Mercado Pago (FR-ML-007..009)\n\n```\nUser \u2192 /dashboard/settings \u2192 [Conectar MP] \u2192 OAuth redirect \u2192 MP authorize\n  \u2192 callback code \u2192 POST mercado-pago-oauth \u2192 troca code\u2192token\n  \u2192 INSERT mercado_pago_config (user_id=auth.uid())\n  ...\nCliente paga \u2192 MP envia webhook \u2192 mercado-pago-webhook valida x-signature\n  \u2192 processa payment.approved \u2192 persiste status \u2192 pulse infra_health_logs\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. API Contract Definitions\n\n> Toda Edge Function user-facing valida JWT antes de qualquer l\u00f3gica. Webhook MP valida assinatura (n\u00e3o JWT).\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/process-affiliate-link` (estendida)\n\n```typescript\ninterface ProcessAffiliateLinkRequest {\n  product_url?: string\n  item_id?: string\n  campaign_id: string\n  content_id?: string\n}\ninterface ProcessAffiliateLinkResponse {\n  status: 'ok'\n  item_id: string\n  affiliate_url: string   // meli.la real\n  original_url: string\n  link_id: string\n}\n// mcoCoins deduction point:\n// \u2192 ap\u00f3s resolver credencial, antes do call externo:\n//   await supabase.rpc('deduct_mco_coins', { p_user_id, p_amount: 1 })\n// \u2192 HTTP 402 se saldo insuficiente\n```\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/mercado-pago-oauth` (nova)\n\n```typescript\ninterface MpOauthRequest { code: string; redirect_uri: string }\ninterface MpOauthResponse { status: 'ok'; connected: boolean }\n// Sem dedu\u00e7\u00e3o de mcoCoins (onboarding). Token guardado server-side em mercado_pago_config.\n```\n\n### `POST /functions/v1/mercado-pago-webhook` (nova)\n\n```typescript\n// Auth: header x-signature (HMAC MP) \u2014 valida ANTES de processar.\ninterface MpWebhookPayload { type: string; data: { id: string } }\n// Resposta 200 imediata; processamento de payment idempotente por payment.id.\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Security Design (CISSP CBK \u2014 elevated, financial)\n\n### 5.1 Threat Model (STRIDE)\n\n| Component | Threat | Type | Severity | Mitigation |\n|-----------|--------|------|----------|------------|\n| `process-affiliate-link` | Acesso n\u00e3o autenticado | Spoofing | High | Valida\u00e7\u00e3o JWT no entry point |\n| `affiliate_config` | Leitura cross-tenant de credencial | Information Disclosure | Critical | RLS `user_id = auth.uid()` + column-level em `client_secret` |\n| `mercado_pago_config` | Roubo de `access_token` MP | Information Disclosure | Critical | RLS + token server-side only; nunca no bundle (NFR-ML-006) |\n| `mercado-pago-webhook` | Webhook forjado (pagamento falso) | Spoofing/Tampering | Critical | Validar `x-signature` HMAC antes de processar |\n| `deduct_mco_coins` | Bypass client-side de cobran\u00e7a | Tampering | Critical | RPC server-side only |\n| Atribui\u00e7\u00e3o de afiliado | Tenant A usa tag do tenant B | Tampering/Repudiation | High | Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o per-user obrigat\u00f3ria; tag vem de `affiliate_config` do `auth.uid()` |\n| API ML/MP tokens | Vazamento em logs | Information Disclosure | High | Nunca logar token; pulse s\u00f3 status + nota redacted |\n| Postback de receita | Replay de convers\u00e3o (double credit) | Tampering | High | Idempot\u00eancia por `payment_id`/`order_id` |\n\n### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions\n\n```sql\n-- mercado_pago_config RLS (default deny)\nALTER TABLE public.mercado_pago_config ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\nCREATE POLICY \"mp_config_select_own\"\n  ON public.mercado_pago_config FOR SELECT\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid());\n\nCREATE POLICY \"mp_config_insert_own\"\n  ON public.mercado_pago_config FOR INSERT\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n\nCREATE POLICY \"mp_config_update_own\"\n  ON public.mercado_pago_config FOR UPDATE\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid()) WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n\n-- RESTRICTIVE: clientes n\u00e3o podem DELETE (registro financeiro)\nCREATE POLICY \"mp_config_no_delete\"\n  ON public.mercado_pago_config AS RESTRICTIVE FOR DELETE\n  USING (false);\n```\n\n> `access_token`/`refresh_token` em `mercado_pago_config` seguem o mesmo padr\u00e3o column-level de `social_credentials` \u2014 nunca expostos ao client; s\u00f3 edge functions service-role leem.\n\n### 5.3 JWT Validation Points\n\n```typescript\n// Padr\u00e3o em toda edge fn user-facing (process-affiliate-link, mercado-pago-oauth, global-selling-sync):\nconst authHeader = req.headers.get('Authorization')\nif (!authHeader) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })\nconst { data: { user }, error } = await supabase.auth.getUser(authHeader.replace('Bearer ', ''))\nif (error || !user) return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })\n// mercado-pago-webhook N\u00c3O usa JWT \u2014 valida x-signature HMAC.\n```\n\n### 5.4 Secrets Inventory\n\n| Secret Name | Used In | Vault Location | Rotation Policy |\n|-------------|---------|----------------|----------------|\n| `MP_APP_ID` | `mercado-pago-oauth` | Supabase Vault | Onboarding/fallback; rotacionar anual |\n| `MP_CLIENT_SECRET` | `mercado-pago-oauth` | Supabase Vault | Rotacionar 90d / on-incident |\n| `MP_WEBHOOK_SECRET` | `mercado-pago-webhook` | Supabase Vault | Rotacionar on-incident |\n| (per-user) `affiliate_config.app_id/client_secret` | `process-affiliate-link` | Tabela (RLS) | Gerenciado pelo usu\u00e1rio |\n| (per-user) `mercado_pago_config.access_token` | MP fns | Tabela (RLS) | Refresh token autom\u00e1tico |\n\n> \u26a0\ufe0f Per **API Tenancy Model**: credenciais ML/MP de fluxos user-facing resolvem **per-user primeiro**. `MP_APP_ID`/`MP_CLIENT_SECRET` no vault s\u00e3o fallback de onboarding (default app), n\u00e3o o caminho prim\u00e1rio de atribui\u00e7\u00e3o.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Infrastructure Touchpoints\n\n| Infrastructure | Usage | Impact |\n|----------------|-------|--------|\n| `infra_health_logs` | Novos `service`: `mercado-pago`, `global-selling-sync` (+ `affiliate-link-resolution` j\u00e1 existe) | Watchdog telemetry |\n| `mega-brain-daemon` | Detecta error-rate alto em fns ML/MP \u2192 self-heal redeploy | Auto-recovery |\n| `mcorch_chroma` | N\u00e3o usado diretamente (sem busca vetorial neste m\u00f3dulo) | Nenhum |\n| `mcorch_nodes`/`mcorch_edges` | Observation node por convers\u00e3o + edge `ATTRIBUTES_REVENUE_TO` | GraphRAG |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Migration Stubs\n\n```sql\n-- Migration: <ts>_mercado_pago_config.sql\nCREATE TABLE public.mercado_pago_config (\n  id             uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  user_id        uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  mp_user_id     text,                       -- collector_id MP\n  access_token   text NOT NULL,              -- server-side only\n  refresh_token  text,\n  public_key     text,\n  expires_at     timestamptz,\n  is_active      boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\n  created_at     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  UNIQUE (user_id)\n);\nALTER TABLE public.mercado_pago_config ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n-- (pol\u00edticas RLS de \u00a75.2)\nCREATE INDEX mercado_pago_config_user_idx ON public.mercado_pago_config (user_id);\n\n-- Migration: <ts>_global_selling_currency.sql\nALTER TABLE public.vm_affiliate_products\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS currency text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'BRL';\n-- CHECK opcional: currency IN ('BRL','USD')\n```\n\n> Toda migration nova **passa por `/security-review` antes do commit** (FMEA-011, regra CLAUDE.md).\n\n---\n\n## 8. Open Technical Decisions\n\n| ID | Description | Option A | Option B | Recommendation | Owner |\n|----|-------------|----------|----------|----------------|-------|\n| OTD-ML-001 | Endpoint exato de gera\u00e7\u00e3o de short link de afiliado (`meli.la`) | API oficial ML Afiliados (confirmar path em developers.mercadolivre.com.br) | Gera\u00e7\u00e3o via redirect pr\u00f3prio com tag em query param | **A** \u2014 validar materialmente a doc oficial ANTES de codar (Lei 1); s\u00f3 cair em B se A n\u00e3o existir publicamente | Sovereign |\n| OTD-ML-002 | Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o da tag `caga6077534` no multi-tenant | Coluna nova `affiliate_config.affiliate_tag` per-user | Derivar da conta OAuth do afiliado | **A** \u2014 expl\u00edcito e audit\u00e1vel per-user | Engineering |\n| OTD-ML-003 | Mercado Pago: OAuth Connect (marketplace) vs credencial direta | OAuth Connect (token por seller) | Colar access_token manualmente | **A** para escala; **B** como fallback de onboarding r\u00e1pido | Sovereign |\n| OTD-ML-004 | Postback ML: idempot\u00eancia | Por `order_id` ML | Por `(content_id, timestamp)` | **A** \u2014 `order_id` \u00e9 \u00fanico e \u00e0 prova de replay | Engineering |\n| OTD-ML-005 | Global Selling: fonte de cat\u00e1logo | API Global Selling dedicada | Filtro `currency` sobre search normal | Confirmar disponibilidade da API; default **B** se A indispon\u00edvel | Sovereign |\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

O conhecimento entra via `mcorch_nodes` (observa\u00e7\u00e3o de convers\u00e3o).\n\n---\n\n## 4. GraphRAG Knowledge Nodes\n\n### Node Types Introduced\n\n| node_type | Title Pattern | Content Fields | Embedding Source |\n|-----------|--------------|----------------|-----------------|\n| `observation` | `ml-conversion-<order_id>` | resumo da convers\u00e3o (produto, valor, campanha) | content (title + summary) |\n| `documentation_suite` | `bok-scribe-seal-mercado-livre-api-v1` | s\u00edntese da BoK | content |\n\n> Este m\u00f3dulo N\u00c3O cria um novo `node_type` dedicado \u2014 reusa `observation` (convers\u00e3o) e `documentation_suite` (seal), consistente com o padr\u00e3o de afiliados existente.\n\n### Edge Types Introduced / Reused\n\n| Edge relationship | Source node_type | Target node_type | Metadata |\n|-------------------|-----------------|-----------------|----------|\n| `ATTRIBUTES_REVENUE_TO` (reuso OE-661) | `observation` (convers\u00e3o) | tenant/feature node | `{ revenue_cents, order_id }` |\n| `CONTAINS_LINK_FOR` (reuso) | `content_mesh_asset` | produto/link | `{ link_id }` |\n| `DERIVES_FROM` | seal `mercado-livre-api` | seal `viralmind` | \u2014 |\n\n### Embedding Trigger\n\n```typescript\n// Ap\u00f3s postback de convers\u00e3o bem-sucedido, opcionalmente registrar observa\u00e7\u00e3o:\nawait supabase.functions.invoke('embed-mcorch-node', {\n  body: { node_id: observationNode.id, content: `${title} ${summary}` }\n})\n// Ou via trg_mcorch_embed_on_insert (pg_net) no INSERT.\n```\n\n### FSRS-6 Parameters\n\n| Field | Initial Value | Decay Rule |\n|-------|--------------|------------|\n| `stability_score` | `0.85` (seal) / `0.5` (observa\u00e7\u00e3o) | migration `20260425190000` |\n| `revenue_impact` | derivado do `revenue_cents` agregado da campanha | atualizado por postback |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Tenant Isolation Map\n\n| Table | `user_id` scope | `project_id` scope | System rows (user_id IS NULL) |\n|-------|----------------|-------------------|-------------------------------|\n| `affiliate_config` | \u2705 Required (UNIQUE user+platform) | \u2014 | No |\n| `mercado_pago_config` | \u2705 Required (UNIQUE user) | \u2014 | No |\n| `affiliate_links` | \u2705 Required | Optional (`campaign_id`) | No |\n| `vm_affiliate_products` | \u2705 Required | \u2014 | No |\n| `mcorch_nodes` (observa\u00e7\u00e3o) | Optional | `mcorch-affiliate` | Sometimes |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Data Quality Rules\n\n| Table | Column | Rule | Violation Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|--------|------|--------------------------|\n| `affiliate_links` | `short_url` | deve iniciar com `meli.la` ou dom\u00ednio oficial; nunca `panel.gcrux.com` nem MLB fabricado | \"Link de afiliado inv\u00e1lido \u2014 n\u00e3o foi gerado pela API oficial.\" |\n| `affiliate_links` | `metadata.item_id` | regex `^MLB\\d{6,}$` (item real resolvido) | \"Item Mercado Livre n\u00e3o resolvido.\" |\n| `mercado_pago_config` | `access_token` | n\u00e3o-vazio quando `is_active` | \"Token Mercado Pago ausente \u2014 reconecte.\" |\n| `vm_affiliate_products` | `currency` | IN ('BRL','USD') | \"Moeda n\u00e3o suportada.\" |\n| `affiliate_config` | `affiliate_tag` | n\u00e3o-vazio para gerar link atribu\u00edvel | \"Tag de afiliado n\u00e3o configurada.\" |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Retention & Archival Policy\n\n| Data | Retention Period | Archival Strategy | Deletion Trigger |\n|------|-----------------|-------------------|-----------------|\n| `affiliate_links` rows | Indefinido (hist\u00f3rico de receita) | \u2014 | Com a conta (LGPD `delete_account()`) |\n| `mercado_pago_config` | Enquanto conta ativa | \u2014 | LGPD delete / revoga\u00e7\u00e3o OAuth |\n| MP payment notifications | Log transiente; status persistido em `affiliate_links`/futuro | \u2014 | Cron purge 90d (logs) |\n| `infra_health_logs` (ML/MP) | 90 dias | Sem arquivamento | Cron purge |\n| Embeddings | Indefinido | `scripts/backfill-embeddings.ts` | Com o n\u00f3 pai |\n\n> **LGPD:** ambas as tabelas de credencial entram no escopo do `delete_account()` RPC (DD-001/DD-002) \u2014 o usu\u00e1rio pode revogar/excluir.\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"

                         \u2502 YES                          \u2502 NO\n                       [deduct_mco_coins(1)]              [HTTP 402 ml_affiliate_not_configured]\n degraded]\n                     \u2502                       \u2502\n            [pulse healthy]                 END\n                     \u2502\n            [HTTP 200 {affiliate_url meli.la}]\n                     \u2502\n                    END\n```\n\n**Swimlanes:**\n\n| Swimlane | Participant | Tasks |\n|----------|-------------|-------|\n| User | Browser / `AffiliateProductsPage` | Aciona \"Monetizar Links ML\" |\n| Frontend Hook | `useAffiliateProducts` | POST \u2192 invalida cache ao sucesso |\n| Edge Function | `process-affiliate-link` | JWT, credencial per-user, deduct, resolve item, gera link, persiste, pulse |\n| External API | `api.mercadolibre.com` | `/items/{id}`, search, afiliados |\n| DB | Supabase | `affiliate_config` (read), `affiliate_links` (write), `deduct_mco_coins` RPC |\n\n---\n\n### PROC-ML-004: Webhook de pagamento Mercado Pago\n\n```\n[MP envia notifica\u00e7\u00e3o] \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [mercado-pago-webhook]\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Validar x-signature HMAC \u2500\u2500(falha)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 HTTP 401 + pulse error (forjado)\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 ACK HTTP 200 imediato (idempotente por payment.id)\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 [GET /v1/payments/{id} via token per-user do collector]\n  \u2502       \u2502\n  \u2502       \u2514\u2500\u2500(status=approved)\u2500\u2500\u25b6 [persistir status + pulse healthy]\n  \u2502       \u2514\u2500\u2500(status=rejected)\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6 [persistir + pulse degraded]\n  \u2502\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 [Notificar usu\u00e1rio (PT-BR) \u2014 opcional]\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. n8n Workflow Blueprints\n\n### Workflow: Global Selling catalog refresh (opcional/cron)\n\n**Trigger:** cron di\u00e1rio OU bot\u00e3o manual\n**Idempotency key:** `global-selling-sync:<user_id>:<yyyymmdd>`\n\n```\n[Trigger: cron di\u00e1rio 06:00 BRT]\n  \u2502\n  \u25bc\n[Node: HTTP Request \u2192 /functions/v1/global-selling-sync]\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Headers: Authorization: Bearer <user_jwt OU service-role para cron>\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 Body: { currency: \"USD\", limit: 50 }\n  \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500 (success) \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [Node: upsert vm_affiliate_products (currency=USD)]\n  \u2502                         \u2502\n  \u2502                         \u25bc\n  \u2502                 [Node: pulse infra_health_logs healthy]\n  \u2502\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500 (error) \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [Node: retry count + 1]\n                         \u2502\n                   (count \u2264 3) \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [Wait: backoff 2/4/8s] \u2500\u2500\u25b6 retry\n                         \u2502\n                   (count > 3) \u2500\u2500\u25b6 [Node: infra_health_logs {service:'global-selling-sync', status:'error'}]\n                                         \u2502\n                                   [Node: Alert Sovereign (Telegram via Guardian)]\n```\n\n**Data Mapping:**\n\n| Input Field | Source | \u2192 | Output Field | Destination |\n|-------------|--------|---|--------------|-------------|\n| `currency` | Trigger body | \u2192 | filtro de busca | ML Global Selling API |\n| `user token` | `affiliate_config`/per-user | \u2192 | `Authorization` | ML API header |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Orchestration Integration Points\n\n> `process-affiliate-link` j\u00e1 \u00e9 invocado no passo 5 do `orchestrate-content` (anexar links de afiliado + UTM). Este m\u00f3dulo corrige o link gerado nesse passo.\n\n| Pipeline Step | Step ID | Position | Description | mcoCoins Trigger |\n|---------------|---------|----------|-------------|-----------------|\n| Anexar link de afiliado ML real | step_5 | Ap\u00f3s `publish-wordpress` | Resolve produto + gera `meli.la` por `auth.uid()` | 1 coin/link no entry |\n\n**Integration code stub (dentro de `orchestrate-content/index.ts`, passo 5):**\n```typescript\n// Step 5: attach ML affiliate links (real meli.la, per-user)\nawait updateNodeStatus(nodes, 'step_5', 'running')\nconst linkResult = await callProcessAffiliateLink({ product_url, campaign_id, content_id })\nif (!linkResult.ok || !linkResult.affiliate_url?.startsWith('meli.la')) {\n  await updateNodeStatus(nodes, 'step_5', 'error')   // n\u00e3o publica link 404\n  // degrade gracefully: publica conte\u00fado sem link em vez de link quebrado\n}\nawait updateNodeStatus(nodes, 'step_5', 'complete')\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Error Handling & Retry Strategy\n\n| Process | Step | Error Type | Max Retries | Backoff | Failure Action |\n|---------|------|-----------|-------------|---------|----------------|\n| PROC-ML-001 | resolve item | ML 5xx / timeout | 3 | Exp (2/4/8s) | pulse degraded \u2192 HTTP 422 |\n| PROC-ML-001 | gerar link | ML afiliados 5xx | 2 | Fixo 5s | pulse error \u2192 fallback: n\u00e3o emite link quebrado |\n| PROC-ML-001 | credencial ausente | config not found | 0 | \u2014 | HTTP 402 fail-closed |\n| PROC-ML-001 | mcoCoins | saldo insuficiente | 0 | \u2014 | HTTP 402 |\n| PROC-ML-004 | webhook | assinatura inv\u00e1lida | 0 | \u2014 | HTTP 401 + pulse error |\n| PROC-ML-004 | buscar payment | MP 5xx | 3 | Exp | retry; ap\u00f3s esgotar, log + reprocessa via cron |\n| Any | JWT | token inv\u00e1lido | 0 | \u2014 | HTTP 401 imediato |\n\n---\n\n## 6. SLA Definitions\n\n| Process | Step | Expected Duration | Timeout Threshold | Alert if exceeded |\n|---------|------|------------------|-------------------|-------------------|\n| PROC-ML-001 | End-to-end | < 1.5s | 8s | Sim \u2192 infra_health_logs |\n| PROC-ML-001 | resolve item | < 600ms | 4s | N\u00e3o |\n| PROC-ML-002 | postback | < 800ms | 5s | Sim |\n| PROC-ML-004 | webhook ack | < 300ms | 2s | Sim (MP re-tenta se demorar) |\n| PROC-ML-005 | global sync | < 10s | 30s | Sim |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Cron Jobs\n\n| Job Name | Schedule (cron) | Function | Idempotency | Failure Alerting |\n|----------|----------------|----------|-------------|-----------------|\n| `global-selling-refresh` | `0 9 * * *` (06h BRT) | `global-selling-sync` | `global-selling-sync:<user>:<date>` | `infra_health_logs` + Guardian Telegram |\n| `mp-payment-reconcile` | `*/30 * * * *` | reprocessa payments pendentes | `mp-reconcile:<payment_id>` | `infra_health_logs` |\n\n> Todos os crons idempotentes \u2014 re-rodar sobre o mesmo dado produz o mesmo resultado. Preservar crons existentes (watchdog `*/5`, auto-publish `*/5`, audit-4cs, ast-ingest, nightly-bridge 02:30, dreaming 03:00, briefing 03:30).\n\n---\n\n_Generated by MCORCH BoK Scribe Agent v1.0.0_\n"
